'Cortical subplate']\n166\t_OLFACTORY = [\n167\t    'Anterior olfactory nucleus', 'Piriform area', 'Olfactory areas', 'Taenia tecta',\n168\t    'Accessory olfactory bulb', 'Main olfactory bulb', 'Dorsal peduncular area',\n169\t    'Nucleus of the lateral olfactory tract', 'Cortical amygdalar area',\n170\t    'Piriform-amygdalar area', 'Postpiriform transition area', 'Olfactory bulb']\n171\t_ORBITAL = ['Orbital area']\n172\t_CORTEX = [\n173\t    'Primary motor area', 'Secondary motor area', 'Primary somatosensory area',\n174\t    'Supplemental somatosensory area', 'Gustatory areas', 'Visceral area',\n175\t    'Agranular insular area', 'Anterior cingulate area', 'Prelimbic area', 'Infralimbic area',\n176\t    'Frontal pole', 'Retrosplenial area', 'Temporal association areas', 'Perirhinal area',\n177\t    'Ectorhinal area', 'Primary auditory area', 'Dorsal auditory area', 'Ventral auditory area',\n178\t    'Posterior auditory area', 'Primary visual area', 'posteromedial visual area',\n179\t    'Anteromedial visual area', 'Anterolateral visual area', 'Lateral visual area',\n180\t    'Laterointermediate area', 'Rostrolateral area', 'Posterolateral visual area',\n181\t    'Anterior area', 'Postrhinal area', 'Isocortex', 'Somatomotor areas',\n182\t    'Somatosensory areas', 'Auditory areas', 'Visual areas',\n183\t    'Posterior parietal association areas', 'Cerebral cortex']\n184\t\n185\t_GROUPS = [('Pons', _PONS), ('Medulla', _MEDULLA), ('Cerebellum', _CEREBELLUM),\n186\t           ('Hypothalamus', _HYPOTHALAMUS), ('Thalamus', _THALAMUS), ('Midbrain', _MIDBRAIN),\n187\t           ('Pallidum', _PALLIDUM), ('Striatum', _STRIATUM), ('Hippocampus', _HIPPOCAMPUS),\n188\t           ('CorticalSubplate', _CTXSP), ('Olfactory', _OLFACTORY), ('Orbital', _ORBITAL),\n189\t           ('OtherCortex', _CORTEX)]\n190\t\n191\t# cortical annotations that can belong to ALM (anterior-lateral motor cortex)\n192\t_ALM_CANDIDATE_PREFIXES = ('Primary motor area', 'Secondary motor area', 'Frontal pole')\n193\t\n194\t\n195\tdef annotation_to_group(name):\n196\t    \"\"\"Map a single CCF annotation string to one of the 14 major groups (or None).\"\"\"\n197\t    for gname, keys in _GROUPS:\n198\t        for k in keys:\n199\t            if k in name:\n200\t                return gname\n201\t    return None\n202\t\n203\t\n204\tdef assign_brain_regions(anno, ccf_ap):\n205\t    \"\"\"Assign each unit to one of BRAIN_REGIONS.\n206\t\n207\t    Args:\n208\t        anno: (n_units,) array of CCF annotation strings.\n209\t        ccf_ap: (n_units,) CCF AP coordinate (micrometres, Allen CCFv3 `z`).\n210\t\n211\t    Returns:\n212\t        (n_units,) int array of indices into BRAIN_REGIONS.\n213\t    \"\"\"\n214\t    ap_mm = (CCF_BREGMA_AP - np.asarray(ccf_ap, dtype=float)) / 1000.0\n215\t    out = np.empty(len(anno), dtype=np.int64)\n216\t    for i, name in enumerate(anno):\n217\t        grp = annotation_to_group(name)\n218\t        if grp is None:\n219\t            raise ValueError(f'Unmapped CCF annotation: {name!r}')\n220\t        if grp == 'OtherCortex' and name.startswith(_ALM_CANDIDATE_PREFIXES) \\\n221\t                and ap_mm[i] >= ALM_MIN_AP_MM:\n222\t            grp = 'ALM'\n223\t        out[i] = REGION_IDX[grp]\n224\t    return out\n225\t\n226\t\n227\t# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n228\t# Per-session conversion\n229\t# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n230\tdef _decode(arr):\n231\t    return np.array([x.decode() if isinstance(x, bytes) else str(x) for x in arr])\n232\t\n233\t\n234\tdef bin_spikes(spike_times, spike_index, unit_idx, go_times):\n235\t    \"\"\"Bin spikes of the selected units into go-cue-aligned firing rates.\n236\t\n237\t    Mirrors `preprocessing_DJ_2022Aug.sliding_histogram(..., rate=True)` with\n238\t    stride == bin width: spikes are counted in half-open bins [edge_k, edge_{k+1}).\n239\t\n240\t    Args:\n241\t        spike_times: (n_spikes,) concatenated spike times of all units, session time (s).\n242\t        spike_index: (n_units,) end offsets into `spike_times` (NWB ragged index).\n243\t        unit_idx: indices of the units to bin.\n244\t        go_times: (n_trials,) go-cue times of the trials to extract, session time (s).\n245\t\n246\t    Returns:\n247\t        (n_units_sel, n_trials, N_BINS) float32 array of firing rates in spikes/s.\n248\t    \"\"\"\n249\t    n_trials = len(go_times)\n250\t    # absolute bin edges, (n_trials, N_BINS+1)\n251\t    edges = go_times[:, None] + BIN_EDGES[None, :]\n252\t    flat_edges = np.ascontiguousarray(edges.ravel())\n253\t\n254\t    fr = np.empty((len(unit_idx), n_trials, N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n255\t    for i, u in enumerate(unit_idx):\n256\t        lo = spike_index[u - 1] if u > 0 else 0\n257\t        st = spike_times[lo:spike_index[u]]\n258\t        pos = np.searchsorted(st, flat_edges).reshape(n_trials, N_BINS + 1)\n259\t        fr[i] = (pos[:, 1:] - pos[:, :-1]).astype(np.float32)\n260\t    fr /= BIN_SIZE                      # spike counts -> spikes/s\n261\t    return fr\n262\t\n263\t\n264\tdef tongue_per_bin(track_data, track_ts, go_times):\n265\t    \"\"\"Aggregate DeepLabCut tongue tracking into the go-cue-aligned bins.\n266\t\n267\t    Args:\n268\t        track_data: (n_frames, 3) array of (x, y, likelihood).\n269\t        track_ts: (n_frames,) frame times in session time (s), sorted.\n270\t        go_times: (n_trials,) go-cue times.\n271\t\n272\t    Returns:\n273\t        y: (n_trials, N_BINS) mean y over the visible frames of the bin (NaN if none).\n274\t        visible: (n_trials, N_BINS) bool, True if the bin contains a visible frame.\n275\t    \"\"\"\n276\t    n_trials = len(go_times)\n277\t    y = np.full((n_trials, N_BINS), np.nan, dtype=np.float64)\n278\t    visible = np.zeros((n_trials, N_BINS), dtype=bool)\n279\t\n280\t    ycol = track_data[:, 1]\n281\t    vis_all = track_data[:, 2] > TONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESH\n282\t\n283\t    lo = np.searchsorted(track_ts, go_times + OFF_START, side='left')\n284\t    hi = np.searchsorted(track_ts, go_times + OFF_END, side='left')\n285\t    for t in range(n_trials):\n286\t        a, b = lo[t], hi[t]\n287\t        if b <= a:\n288\t            continue\n289\t        rel = track_ts[a:b] - go_times[t]\n290\t        b_idx = np.floor((rel - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE).astype(np.int64)\n291\t        np.clip(b_idx, 0, N_BINS - 1, out=b_idx)\n292\t        v = vis_all[a:b]\n293\t        if not v.any():\n294\t            continue\n295\t        cnt = np.bincount(b_idx[v], minlength=N_BINS)\n296\t        tot = np.bincount(b_idx[v], weights=ycol[a:b][v], minlength=N_BINS)\n297\t        m = cnt > 0\n298\t        y[t, m] = tot[m] / cnt[m]\n299\t        visible[t, m] = True\n300\t    return y, visible\n301\t\n302\t\n303\tdef discretize_tongue(y, visible):\n304\t    \"\"\"Discretise per-bin tongue y-position using per-session percentiles.\n305\t\n306\t    0: y < 40th percentile, 1: 40th-60th percentile, 2: y > 60th percentile,\n307\t    3: tongue not visible.  Percentiles are taken over the visible bins of the session\n308\t    (y is meaningless where the tongue is not visible).\n309\t    \"\"\"\n310\t    cls = np.full(y.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)\n311\t    if visible.any():\n312\t        vals = y[visible]\n313\t        p40, p60 = np.percentile(vals, TONGUE_PCTL)\n314\t        cls[visible & (y < p40)] = 0\n315\t        cls[visible & (y >= p40) & (y <= p60)] = 1\n316\t        cls[visible & (y > p60)] = 2\n317\t    else:\n318\t        p40 = p60 = np.nan\n319\t    return cls, (p40, p60)\n320\t\n321\t\n322\tdef process_session(fname, want_raw=False):\n323\t    \"\"\"Convert a single NWB session file.\n324\t\n325\t    Returns None if the session has no usable units/trials.\n326\t    \"\"\"\n327\t    t0 = time.time()\n328\t    with h5py.File(fname, 'r') as f:\n329\t        ident = f['identifier'][()].decode()\n330\t        mouse = f['general/subject/description'][()].decode().strip()\n331\t        subject_id = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode().strip()\n332\t\n333\t        tr = f['intervals/trials']\n334\t        n_trials_table = len(tr['id'])\n335\t        start_time = tr['start_time'][:]\n336\t        stop_time = tr['stop_time'][:]\n337\t        outcome = _decode(tr['outcome'][:])\n338\t        early = _decode(tr['early_lick'][:])\n339\t        instruction = _decode(tr['trial_instruction'][:])\n340\t        auto_water = tr['auto_water'][:]\n341\t        free_water = tr['free_water'][:]\n342\t        ps_onset = _decode(tr['photostim_onset'][:])\n343\t        ps_dur = _decode(tr['photostim_duration'][:])\n344\t\n345\t        go = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/go_start_times/timestamps'][:]\n346\t        sample_start = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/sample_start_times/timestamps'][:]\n347\t        assert len(go) == n_trials_table, f'{ident}: go cue count != trial count'\n348\t\n349\t        # ---- units --------------------------------------------------------------\n350\t        u = f['units']\n351\t        classification = _decode(u['classification'][:])\n352\t        anno = _decode(u['anno_name'][:])\n353\t        keep = (classification == 'good') & (anno != '')\n354\t        unit_idx = np.where(keep)[0]\n355\t        if len(unit_idx) == 0:\n356\t            return None\n357\t\n358\t        # CCF coordinates of each unit's electrode\n359\t        el = u['electrodes'][:]\n360\t        etab = f['general/extracellular_ephys/electrodes']\n361\t        ccf_ml = etab['x'][:][el[unit_idx]]\n362\t        ccf_ap = etab['z'][:][el[unit_idx]]\n363\t\n364\t        # ---- which trials did the ephys recording actually cover? ---------------\n365\t        oii = u['obs_intervals_index'][:]\n366\t        n_obs = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], oii]))\n367\t        n_obs_good = n_obs[unit_idx]\n368\t        observed = np.zeros(n_trials_table, dtype=bool)\n369\t        if np.all(n_obs_good == n_trials_table):\n370\t            observed[:] = True\n371\t        else:\n372\t            # all units of a session share the same contiguous observed trial set;\n373\t            # derive it from the union of their observation intervals to be safe.\n374\t            oi = u['obs_intervals']\n375\t            for k in unit_idx:\n376\t                o = oi[(oii[k] - n_obs[k]):oii[k]]\n377\t                idx = np.searchsorted(start_time, o[:, 0] + 1e-9) - 1\n378\t                observed[idx[idx >= 0]] = True\n379\t\n380\t        # ---- trial curation ------------------------------------------------------\n381\t        valid = observed & (auto_water == 0) & (free_water == 0)\n382\t        trials = np.where(valid)[0]\n383\t        if len(trials) < 2:\n384\t            return None\n385\t\n386\t        go_v = go[trials]\n387\t\n388\t        # ---- neural --------------------------------------------------------------\n389\t        spike_index = u['spike_times_index'][:]\n390\t        spike_times = u['spike_times'][:]\n391\t        fr = bin_spikes(spike_times, spike_index, unit_idx, go_v)\n392\t        del spike_times\n393\t\n394\t        # Drop trials in which not one of the (>=90) simultaneously recorded neurons\n395\t        # fired a single spike in the whole 4 s window.  This is a recording-coverage\n396\t        # failure, not biology: in this release `units/obs_intervals` occasionally\n397\t        # lists one more trial than the spike record actually extends to (e.g.\n398\t        # SC015_20190208_133600 trial 159, whose window starts after the last spike\n399\t        # in the file).\n400\t        has_data = fr.sum(axis=(0, 2)) > 0\n401\t        n_zero_trials = int((~has_data).sum())\n402\t        if n_zero_trials:\n403\t            fr = fr[:, has_data, :]\n404\t            trials = trials[has_data]\n405\t            go_v = go_v[has_data]\n406\t            if len(trials) < 2:\n407\t                return None\n408\t\n409\t        # ---- inputs --------------------------------------------------------------\n410\t        # tone onset: last sample-epoch start before the go cue of that trial\n411\t        j = np.searchsorted(sample_start, go_v - 1e-9) - 1\n412\t        assert np.all(j >= 0), f'{ident}: trial without a preceding tone onset'\n413\t        tone_time = sample_start[j]\n414\t        # time from tone onset at every bin centre, (n_trials, N_BINS)\n415\t        time_from_tone = (BIN_CENTERS[None, :] + (go_v - tone_time)[:, None]).astype(np.float32)\n416\t\n417\t        photostim = np.zeros((len(trials), N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n418\t        has_ps = ps_onset[trials] != 'N/A'\n419\t        if has_ps.any():\n420\t            on = np.array([float(x) for x in ps_onset[trials][has_ps]])\n421\t            dur = np.array([float(x) for x in ps_dur[trials][has_ps]])\n422\t            ps_t0 = start_time[trials][has_ps] + on - go_v[has_ps]   # rel. to go cue\n423\t            ps_t1 = ps_t0 + dur\n424\t            # a bin is \"on\" if it overlaps the stimulation interval\n425\t            ov = (BIN_EDGES[None, :-1] < ps_t1[:, None]) & (BIN_EDGES[None, 1:] > ps_t0[:, None])\n426\t            photostim[has_ps] = ov.astype(np.float32)\n427\t\n428\t        # ---- outputs -------------------------------------------------------------\n429\t        oc = outcome[trials]\n430\t        ins = instruction[trials]\n431\t        choice = np.full(len(trials), 2, dtype=np.int64)              # 2 = no lick\n432\t        licked_left = ((oc == 'hit') & (ins == 'left')) | ((oc == 'miss') & (ins == 'right'))\n433\t        licked_right = ((oc == 'hit') & (ins == 'right')) | ((oc == 'miss') & (ins == 'left'))\n434\t        choice[licked_left] = 0\n435\t        choice[licked_right] = 1\n436\t\n437\t        outcome_c = np.select([oc == 'ignore', oc == 'miss', oc == 'hit'], [0, 1, 2],\n438\t                              default=-1).astype(np.int64)\n439\t        assert np.all(outcome_c >= 0), f'{ident}: unexpected outcome value'\n440\t        early_c = (early[trials] == 'early').astype(np.int64)\n441\t\n442\t        tongue_key = 'acquisition/BehavioralTimeSeries/Camera0_side_TongueTracking'\n443\t        tdata = f[tongue_key + '/data'][:]\n444\t        tts = f[tongue_key + '/timestamps'][:]\n445\t        ty, tvis = tongue_per_bin(tdata, tts, go_v)\n446\t        tongue_c, pctl = discretize_tongue(ty, tvis)\n447\t\n448\t        raw = None\n449\t        if want_raw:\n450\t            raw = dict(track_data=tdata, track_ts=tts, go=go_v, tone=tone_time,\n451\t                       start_time=start_time[trials], stop_time=stop_time[trials],\n452\t                       ty=ty, tvis=tvis, pctl=pctl, outcome=oc, instruction=ins,\n453\t                       spike_index=spike_index, unit_idx=unit_idx, fname=fname)\n454\t\n455\t    region_idx = assign_brain_regions(anno[unit_idx], ccf_ap)\n456\t\n457\t    # ---- assemble ---------------------------------------------------------------\n458\t    n_tr = len(trials)\n459\t    neural = [np.ascontiguousarray(fr[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_tr)]\n460\t    inputs = [np.stack([time_from_tone[t], photostim[t]]) for t in range(n_tr)]\n461\t    outputs = [np.stack([np.full(N_BINS, choice[t]), np.full(N_BINS, outcome_c[t]),\n462\t                         np.full(N_BINS, early_c[t]), tongue_c[t]]).astype(np.int64)\n463\t               for t in range(n_tr)]\n464\t\n465\t    info = dict(\n466\t        session_id=ident, mouse=mouse, subject_id=subject_id, file=os.path.basename(fname),\n467\t        n_units_total=len(classification), n_units_good=int((classification == 'good').sum()),\n468\t        n_neurons=len(unit_idx), n_trials_table=n_trials_table, n_trials=n_tr,\n469\t        n_trials_observed=int(observed.sum()),\n470\t        n_excluded_water=int((observed & ((auto_water != 0) | (free_water != 0))).sum()),\n471\t        n_excluded_no_spikes=n_zero_trials,\n472\t        trial_index=trials.astype(np.int32),   # 0-based index into intervals/trials\n473\t        mean_rate=float(fr.mean()), tongue_pctl=[float(pctl[0]), float(pctl[1])],\n474\t        frac_tongue_visible=float(tvis.mean()),\n475\t        hemisphere_left_frac=float(np.mean(ccf_ml >= CCF_MIDLINE_ML)),\n476\t        proc_time=time.time() - t0,\n477\t    )\n478\t    return dict(neural=neural, input=inputs, output=outputs, region_idx=region_idx,\n479\t                info=info, raw=raw)\n480\t\n481\t\n482\t# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n483\t# Diagnostic plots\n484\t# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n485\tdef make_processing_plot(res, fname_out):\n486\t    import matplotlib\n487\t    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n488\t    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n489\t\n490\t    raw = res['raw']\n491\t    info = res['info']\n492\t    neural, inputs, outputs = res['neural'], res['input'], res['output']\n493\t    n_tr = len(neural)\n494\t    tt = BIN_CENTERS\n495\t\n496\t    # pick a trial that has a visible tongue and (if possible) photostimulation\n497\t    score = np.array([(outputs[t][3] < 3).sum() + 100 * (inputs[t][1].max() > 0)\n498\t                      for t in range(n_tr)])\n499\t    ti = int(np.argmax(score))\n500\t\n501\t    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(22, 18))\n502\t\n503\t    # (0,0) raw spike raster + binned rates for the example trial ------------------\n504\t    a = ax[0, 0]\n505\t    with h5py.File(raw['fname'], 'r') as f:\n506\t        st_all = f['units/spike_times']\n507\t        si = raw['spike_index']\n508\t        nshow = min(25, len(raw['unit_idx']))\n509\t        sel = np.linspace(0, len(raw['unit_idx']) - 1, nshow).astype(int)\n510\t        for row, k in enumerate(sel):\n511\t            uu = raw['unit_idx'][k]\n512\t            lo = si[uu - 1] if uu > 0 else 0\n513\t            s = st_all[lo:si[uu]]\n514\t            s = s[(s >= raw['go'][ti] + OFF_START) & (s < raw['go'][ti] + OFF_END)] - raw['go'][ti]\n515\t            a.plot(s, np.full(len(s), row), '|', color='k', ms=4)\n516\t            a.step(tt, row + 0.8 * neural[ti][k] / max(1e-9, neural[ti][k].max()),\n517\t                   where='mid', color='tab:blue', lw=0.8)\n518\t    a.axvline(0, color='r', lw=1.5, label='go cue')\n519\t    a.axvline(raw['tone'][ti] - raw['go'][ti], color='g', lw=1.5, label='tone onset')\n520\t    a.axvline(raw['start_time'][ti] - raw['go'][ti], color='gray', ls='--', label='trial start')\n521\t    a.axvline(raw['stop_time'][ti] - raw['go'][ti], color='gray', ls=':', label='trial stop')\n522\t    a.set_xlim(OFF_START, OFF_END)\n523\t    a.set_title(f'{info[\"session_id\"]} trial {ti}: raw spikes (ticks) vs binned rate (blue)')\n524\t    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.set_ylabel('neuron'); a.legend(fontsize=7)\n525\t\n526\t    # (0,1) neural heatmap ---------------------------------------------------------\n527\t    a = ax[0, 1]\n528\t    im = a.imshow(neural[ti], aspect='auto', origin='lower',\n529\t                  extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, neural[ti].shape[0]], cmap='magma')\n530\t    a.axvline(0, color='c', lw=1.5)\n531\t    a.set_title(f'binned firing rate (spikes/s), trial {ti}, n={neural[ti].shape[0]} neurons')\n532\t    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.set_ylabel('neuron')\n533\t    fig.colorbar(im, ax=a)\n534\t\n535\t    # (1,0) inputs for the example trial -------------------------------------------\n536\t    a = ax[1, 0]\n537\t    a.plot(tt, inputs[ti][0], '-o', ms=3, label='time from tone onset (s)')\n538\t    a.plot(tt, inputs[ti][1], '-s', ms=3, label='photostim on')\n539\t    a.axvline(0, color='r', lw=1.5)\n540\t    a.axvline(raw['tone'][ti] - raw['go'][ti], color='g', lw=1.5)\n541\t    a.axhline(0, color='k', lw=0.5)\n542\t    a.set_title('decoder inputs (green = tone onset, red = go cue); input 0 must cross 0 at green')\n543\t    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.legend()\n544\t\n545\t    # (1,1) photostim raster across trials -----------------------------------------\n546\t    a = ax[1, 1]\n547\t    ps = np.array([inputs[t][1] for t in range(n_tr)])\n548\t    a.imshow(ps, aspect='auto', origin='lower', extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, n_tr],\n549\t             cmap='Greys', interpolation='nearest')\n550\t    a.axvline(0, color='r', lw=1.5)\n551\t    a.set_title(f'photostim input, all trials ({int((ps.max(1) > 0).sum())} stim trials)')\n552\t    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.set_ylabel('trial')\n553\t\n554\t    # (2,0) tongue tracking, raw vs binned vs discretised --------------------------\n555\t    a = ax[2, 0]\n556\t    m = (raw['track_ts'] >= raw['go'][ti] + OFF_START) & (raw['track_ts'] < raw['go'][ti] + OFF_END)\n557\t    rel = raw['track_ts'][m] - 'Intercalated amygdalar nucleus',\n    'Medial amygdalar nucleus', 'Anterior amygdalar area', 'Septohippocampal nucleus',\n    'Striatum-like amygdalar nuclei', 'Striatum dorsal region', 'Striatum ventral region',\n    'Lateral septal complex', 'Islands of Calleja', 'Major island of Calleja',\n    'Septofimbrial nucleus']\n_PALLIDUM = [\n    'Globus pallidus', 'Pallidum', 'Substantia innominata',\n    'Bed nuclei of the stria terminalis', 'Triangular nucleus of septum',\n    'Medial septal nucleus', 'Diagonal band nucleus', 'Magnocellular nucleus',\n    'Bed nucleus of the anterior commissure']\n_HIPPOCAMPUS = [\n    'Field CA1', 'Field CA2', 'Field CA3', 'Dentate gyrus', 'Subiculum', 'Postsubiculum',\n    'Presubiculum', 'Parasubiculum', 'Entorhinal area', 'Hippocampal formation',\n    'Hippocampal region', 'Prosubiculum', 'Induseum griseum', 'Fasciola cinerea', 'Ammon',\n    'Retrohippocampal region', 'Hippocampo-amygdalar transition area', 'Area prostriata']\n_CTXSP = [\n    'Claustrum', 'Endopiriform nucleus', 'Basolateral amygdalar nucleus',\n    'Lateral amygdalar nucleus', 'Basomedial amygdalar nucleus', 'Posterior amygdalar nucleus',\n    'Cortical subplate']\n_OLFACTORY = [\n    'Anterior olfactory nucleus', 'Piriform area', 'Olfactory areas', 'Taenia tecta',\n    'Accessory olfactory bulb', 'Main olfactory bulb', 'Dorsal peduncular area',\n    'Nucleus of the lateral olfactory tract', 'Cortical amygdalar area',\n    'Piriform-amygdalar area', 'Postpiriform transition area', 'Olfactory bulb']\n_ORBITAL = ['Orbital area']\n_CORTEX = [\n    'Primary motor area', 'Secondary motor area', 'Primary somatosensory area',\n    'Supplemental somatosensory area', 'Gustatory areas', 'Visceral area',\n    'Agranular insular area', 'Anterior cingulate area', 'Prelimbic area', 'Infralimbic area',\n    'Frontal pole', 'Retrosplenial area', 'Temporal association areas', 'Perirhinal area',\n    'Ectorhinal area', 'Primary auditory area', 'Dorsal auditory area', 'Ventral auditory area',\n    'Posterior auditory area', 'Primary visual area', 'posteromedial visual area',\n    'Anteromedial visual area', 'Anterolateral visual area', 'Lateral visual area',\n    'Laterointermediate area', 'Rostrolateral area', 'Posterolateral visual area',\n    'Anterior area', 'Postrhinal area', 'Isocortex', 'Somatomotor areas',\n    'Somatosensory areas', 'Auditory areas', 'Visual areas',\n    'Posterior parietal association areas', 'Cerebral cortex']\n\n_GROUPS = [('Pons', _PONS), ('Medulla', _MEDULLA), ('Cerebellum', _CEREBELLUM),\n           ('Hypothalamus', _HYPOTHALAMUS), ('Thalamus', _THALAMUS), ('Midbrain', _MIDBRAIN),\n           ('Pallidum', _PALLIDUM), ('Striatum', _STRIATUM), ('Hippocampus', _HIPPOCAMPUS),\n           ('CorticalSubplate', _CTXSP), ('Olfactory', _OLFACTORY), ('Orbital', _ORBITAL),\n           ('OtherCortex', _CORTEX)]\n\n# cortical annotations that can belong to ALM (anterior-lateral motor cortex)\n_ALM_CANDIDATE_PREFIXES = ('Primary motor area', 'Secondary motor area', 'Frontal pole')\n\n\ndef annotation_to_group(name):\n    \"\"\"Map a single CCF annotation string to one of the 14 major groups (or None).\"\"\"\n    for gname, keys in _GROUPS:\n        for k in keys:\n            if k in name:\n                return gname\n    return None\n\n\ndef assign_brain_regions(anno, ccf_ap):\n    \"\"\"Assign each unit to one of BRAIN_REGIONS.\n\n    Args:\n        anno: (n_units,) array of CCF annotation strings.\n        ccf_ap: (n_units,) CCF AP coordinate (micrometres, Allen CCFv3 `z`).\n\n    Returns:\n        (n_units,) int array of indices into BRAIN_REGIONS.\n    \"\"\"\n    ap_mm = (CCF_BREGMA_AP - np.asarray(ccf_ap, dtype=float)) / 1000.0\n    out = np.empty(len(anno), dtype=np.int64)\n    for i, name in enumerate(anno):\n        grp = annotation_to_group(name)\n        if grp is None:\n            raise ValueError(f'Unmapped CCF annotation: {name!r}')\n        if grp == 'OtherCortex' and name.startswith(_ALM_CANDIDATE_PREFIXES) \\\n                and ap_mm[i] >= ALM_MIN_AP_MM:\n            grp = 'ALM'\n        out[i] = REGION_IDX[grp]\n    return out\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Per-session conversion\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef _decode(arr):\n    return np.array([x.decode() if isinstance(x, bytes) else str(x) for x in arr])\n\n\ndef bin_spikes(spike_times, spike_index, unit_idx, go_times):\n    \"\"\"Bin spikes of the selected units into go-cue-aligned firing rates.\n\n    Mirrors `preprocessing_DJ_2022Aug.sliding_histogram(..., rate=True)` with\n    stride == bin width: spikes are counted in half-open bins [edge_k, edge_{k+1}).\n\n    Args:\n        spike_times: (n_spikes,) concatenated spike times of all units, session time (s).\n        spike_index: (n_units,) end offsets into `spike_times` (NWB ragged index).\n        unit_idx: indices of the units to bin.\n        go_times: (n_trials,) go-cue times of the trials to extract, session time (s).\n\n    Returns:\n        (n_units_sel, n_trials, N_BINS) float32 array of firing rates in spikes/s.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(go_times)\n    # absolute bin edges, (n_trials, N_BINS+1)\n    edges = go_times[:, None] + BIN_EDGES[None, :]\n    flat_edges = np.ascontiguousarray(edges.ravel())\n\n    fr = np.empty((len(unit_idx), n_trials, N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n    for i, u in enumerate(unit_idx):\n        lo = spike_index[u - 1] if u > 0 else 0\n        st = spike_times[lo:spike_index[u]]\n        pos = np.searchsorted(st, flat_edges).reshape(n_trials, N_BINS + 1)\n        fr[i] = (pos[:, 1:] - pos[:, :-1]).astype(np.float32)\n    fr /= BIN_SIZE                      # spike counts -> spikes/s\n    return fr\n\n\ndef tongue_per_bin(track_data, track_ts, go_times):\n    \"\"\"Aggregate DeepLabCut tongue tracking into the go-cue-aligned bins.\n\n    Args:\n        track_data: (n_frames, 3) array of (x, y, likelihood).\n        track_ts: (n_frames,) frame times in session time (s), sorted.\n        go_times: (n_trials,) go-cue times.\n\n    Returns:\n        y: (n_trials, N_BINS) mean y over the visible frames of the bin (NaN if none).\n        visible: (n_trials, N_BINS) bool, True if the bin contains a visible frame.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(go_times)\n    y = np.full((n_trials, N_BINS), np.nan, dtype=np.float64)\n    visible = np.zeros((n_trials, N_BINS), dtype=bool)\n\n    ycol = track_data[:, 1]\n    vis_all = track_data[:, 2] > TONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESH\n\n    lo = np.searchsorted(track_ts, go_times + OFF_START, side='left')\n    hi = np.searchsorted(track_ts, go_times + OFF_END, side='left')\n    for t in range(n_trials):\n        a, b = lo[t], hi[t]\n        if b <= a:\n            continue\n        rel = track_ts[a:b] - go_times[t]\n        b_idx = np.floor((rel - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE).astype(np.int64)\n        np.clip(b_idx, 0, N_BINS - 1, out=b_idx)\n        v = vis_all[a:b]\n        if not v.any():\n            continue\n        cnt = np.bincount(b_idx[v], minlength=N_BINS)\n        tot = np.bincount(b_idx[v], weights=ycol[a:b][v], minlength=N_BINS)\n        m = cnt > 0\n        y[t, m] = tot[m] / cnt[m]\n        visible[t, m] = True\n    return y, visible\n\n\ndef discretize_tongue(y, visible):\n    \"\"\"Discretise per-bin tongue y-position using per-session percentiles.\n\n    0: y < 40th percentile, 1: 40th-60th percentile, 2: y > 60th percentile,\n    3: tongue not visible.  Percentiles are taken over the visible bins of the session\n    (y is meaningless where the tongue is not visible).\n    \"\"\"\n    cls = np.full(y.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)\n    if visible.any():\n        vals = y[visible]\n        p40, p60 = np.percentile(vals, TONGUE_PCTL)\n        cls[visible & (y < p40)] = 0\n        cls[visible & (y >= p40) & (y <= p60)] = 1\n        cls[visible & (y > p60)] = 2\n    else:\n        p40 = p60 = np.nan\n    return cls, (p40, p60)\n\n\ndef process_session(fname, want_raw=False):\n    \"\"\"Convert a single NWB session file.\n\n    Returns None if the session has no usable units/trials.\n    \"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n    with h5py.File(fname, 'r') as f:\n        ident = f['identifier'][()].decode()\n        mouse = f['general/subject/description'][()].decode().strip()\n        subject_id = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode().strip()\n\n        tr = f['intervals/trials']\n        n_trials_table = len(tr['id'])\n        start_time = tr['start_time'][:]\n        stop_time = tr['stop_time'][:]\n        outcome = _decode(tr['outcome'][:])\n        early = _decode(tr['early_lick'][:])\n        instruction = _decode(tr['trial_instruction'][:])\n        auto_water = tr['auto_water'][:]\n        free_water = tr['free_water'][:]\n        ps_onset = _decode(tr['photostim_onset'][:])\n        ps_dur = _decode(tr['photostim_duration'][:])\n\n        go = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/go_start_times/timestamps'][:]\n        sample_start = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/sample_start_times/timestamps'][:]\n        assert len(go) == n_trials_table, f'{ident}: go cue count != trial count'\n\n        # ---- units --------------------------------------------------------------\n        u = f['units']\n        classification = _decode(u['classification'][:])\n        anno = _decode(u['anno_name'][:])\n        keep = (classification == 'good') & (anno != '')\n        unit_idx = np.where(keep)[0]\n        if len(unit_idx) == 0:\n            return None\n\n        # CCF coordinates of each unit's electrode\n        el = u['electrodes'][:]\n        etab = f['general/extracellular_ephys/electrodes']\n        ccf_ml = etab['x'][:][el[unit_idx]]\n        ccf_ap = etab['z'][:][el[unit_idx]]\n\n        # ---- which trials did the ephys recording actually cover? ---------------\n        oii = u['obs_intervals_index'][:]\n        n_obs = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], oii]))\n        n_obs_good = n_obs[unit_idx]\n        observed = np.zeros(n_trials_table, dtype=bool)\n        if np.all(n_obs_good == n_trials_table):\n            observed[:] = True\n        else:\n            # all units of a session share the same contiguous observed trial set;\n            # derive it from the union of their observation intervals to be safe.\n            oi = u['obs_intervals']\n            for k in unit_idx:\n                o = oi[(oii[k] - n_obs[k]):oii[k]]\n                idx = np.searchsorted(start_time, o[:, 0] + 1e-9) - 1\n                observed[idx[idx >= 0]] = True\n\n        # ---- trial curation ------------------------------------------------------\n        valid = observed & (auto_water == 0) & (free_water == 0)\n        trials = np.where(valid)[0]\n        if len(trials) < 2:\n            return None\n\n        go_v = go[trials]\n\n        # ---- neural --------------------------------------------------------------\n        spike_index = u['spike_times_index'][:]\n        spike_times = u['spike_times'][:]\n        fr = bin_spikes(spike_times, spike_index, unit_idx, go_v)\n        del spike_times\n\n        # Drop trials in which not one of the (>=90) simultaneously recorded neurons\n        # fired a single spike in the whole 4 s window.  This is a recording-coverage\n        # failure, not biology: in this release `units/obs_intervals` occasionally\n        # lists one more trial than the spike record actually extends to (e.g.\n        # SC015_20190208_133600 trial 159, whose window starts after the last spike\n        # in the file).\n        has_data = fr.sum(axis=(0, 2)) > 0\n        n_zero_trials = int((~has_data).sum())\n        if n_zero_trials:\n            fr = fr[:, has_data, :]\n            trials = trials[has_data]\n            go_v = go_v[has_data]\n            if len(trials) < 2:\n                return None\n\n        # ---- inputs --------------------------------------------------------------\n        # tone onset: last sample-epoch start before the go cue of that trial\n        j = np.searchsorted(sample_start, go_v - 1e-9) - 1\n        assert np.all(j >= 0), f'{ident}: trial without a preceding tone onset'\n        tone_time = sample_start[j]\n        # time from tone onset at every bin centre, (n_trials, N_BINS)\n        time_from_tone = (BIN_CENTERS[None, :] + (go_v - tone_time)[:, None]).astype(np.float32)\n\n        photostim = np.zeros((len(trials), N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n        has_ps = ps_onset[trials] != 'N/A'\n        if has_ps.any():\n            on = np.array([float(x) for x in ps_onset[trials][has_ps]])\n            dur = np.array([float(x) for x in ps_dur[trials][has_ps]])\n            ps_t0 = start_time[trials][has_ps] + on - go_v[has_ps]   # rel. to go cue\n            ps_t1 = ps_t0 + dur\n            # a bin is \"on\" if it overlaps the stimulation interval\n            ov = (BIN_EDGES[None, :-1] < ps_t1[:, None]) & (BIN_EDGES[None, 1:] > ps_t0[:, None])\n            photostim[has_ps] = ov.astype(np.float32)\n\n        # ---- outputs -------------------------------------------------------------\n        oc = outcome[trials]\n        ins = instruction[trials]\n        choice = np.full(len(trials), 2, dtype=np.int64)              # 2 = no lick\n        licked_left = ((oc == 'hit') & (ins == 'left')) | ((oc == 'miss') & (ins == 'right'))\n        licked_right = ((oc == 'hit') & (ins == 'right')) | ((oc == 'miss') & (ins == 'left'))\n        choice[licked_left] = 0\n        choice[licked_right] = 1\n\n        outcome_c = np.select([oc == 'ignore', oc == 'miss', oc == 'hit'], [0, 1, 2],\n                              default=-1).astype(np.int64)\n        assert np.all(outcome_c >= 0), f'{ident}: unexpected outcome value'\n        early_c = (early[trials] == 'early').astype(np.int64)\n\n        tongue_key = 'acquisition/BehavioralTimeSeries/Camera0_side_TongueTracking'\n        tdata = f[tongue_key + '/data'][:]\n        tts = f[tongue_key + '/timestamps'][:]\n        ty, tvis = tongue_per_bin(tdata, tts, go_v)\n        tongue_c, pctl = discretize_tongue(ty, tvis)\n\n        raw = None\n        if want_raw:\n            raw = dict(track_data=tdata, track_ts=tts, go=go_v, tone=tone_time,\n                       start_time=start_time[trials], stop_time=stop_time[trials],\n                       ty=ty, tvis=tvis, pctl=pctl, outcome=oc, instruction=ins,\n                       spike_index=spike_index, unit_idx=unit_idx, fname=fname)\n\n    region_idx = assign_brain_regions(anno[unit_idx], ccf_ap)\n\n    # ---- assemble ---------------------------------------------------------------\n    n_tr = len(trials)\n    neural = [np.ascontiguousarray(fr[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_tr)]\n    inputs = [np.stack([time_from_tone[t], photostim[t]]) for t in range(n_tr)]\n    outputs = [np.stack([np.full(N_BINS, choice[t]), np.full(N_BINS, outcome_c[t]),\n                         np.full(N_BINS, early_c[t]), tongue_c[t]]).astype(np.int64)\n               for t in range(n_tr)]\n\n    info = dict(\n        session_id=ident, mouse=mouse, subject_id=subject_id, file=os.path.basename(fname),\n        n_units_total=len(classification), n_units_good=int((classification == 'good').sum()),\n        n_neurons=len(unit_idx), n_trials_table=n_trials_table, n_trials=n_tr,\n        n_trials_observed=int(observed.sum()),\n        n_excluded_water=int((observed & ((auto_water != 0) | (free_water != 0))).sum()),\n        n_excluded_no_spikes=n_zero_trials,\n        trial_index=trials.astype(np.int32),   # 0-based index into intervals/trials\n        mean_rate=float(fr.mean()), tongue_pctl=[float(pctl[0]), float(pctl[1])],\n        frac_tongue_visible=float(tvis.mean()),\n        hemisphere_left_frac=float(np.mean(ccf_ml >= CCF_MIDLINE_ML)),\n        proc_time=time.time() - t0,\n    )\n    return dict(neural=neural, input=inputs, output=outputs, region_idx=region_idx,\n                info=info, raw=raw)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Diagnostic plots\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef make_processing_plot(res, fname_out):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    raw = res['raw']\n    info = res['info']\n    neural, inputs, outputs = res['neural'], res['input'], res['output']\n    n_tr = len(neural)\n    tt = BIN_CENTERS\n\n    # pick a trial that has a visible tongue and (if possible) photostimulation\n    score = np.array([(outputs[t][3] < 3).sum() + 100 * (inputs[t][1].max() > 0)\n                      for t in range(n_tr)])\n    ti = int(np.argmax(score))\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(22, 18))\n\n    # (0,0) raw spike raster + binned rates for the example trial ------------------\n    a = ax[0, 0]\n    with h5py.File(raw['fname'], 'r') as f:\n        st_all = f['units/spike_times']\n        si = raw['spike_index']\n        nshow = min(25, len(raw['unit_idx']))\n        sel = np.linspace(0, len(raw['unit_idx']) - 1, nshow).astype(int)\n        for row, k in enumerate(sel):\n            uu = raw['unit_idx'][k]\n            lo = si[uu - 1] if uu > 0 else 0\n            s = st_all[lo:si[uu]]\n            s = s[(s >= raw['go'][ti] + OFF_START) & (s < raw['go'][ti] + OFF_END)] - raw['go'][ti]\n            a.plot(s, np.full(len(s), row), '|', color='k', ms=4)\n            a.step(tt, row + 0.8 * neural[ti][k] / max(1e-9, neural[ti][k].max()),\n                   where='mid', color='tab:blue', lw=0.8)\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', lw=1.5, label='go cue')\n    a.axvline(raw['tone'][ti] - raw['go'][ti], color='g', lw=1.5, label='tone onset')\n    a.axvline(raw['start_time'][ti] - raw['go'][ti], color='gray', ls='--', label='trial start')\n    a.axvline(raw['stop_time'][ti] - raw['go'][ti], color='gray', ls=':', label='trial stop')\n    a.set_xlim(OFF_START, OFF_END)\n    a.set_title(f'{info[\"session_id\"]} trial {ti}: raw spikes (ticks) vs binned rate (blue)')\n    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.set_ylabel('neuron'); a.legend(fontsize=7)\n\n    # (0,1) neural heatmap ---------------------------------------------------------\n    a = ax[0, 1]\n    im = a.imshow(neural[ti], aspect='auto', origin='lower',\n                  extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, neural[ti].shape[0]], cmap='magma')\n    a.axvline(0, color='c', lw=1.5)\n    a.set_title(f'binned firing rate (spikes/s), trial {ti}, n={neural[ti].shape[0]} neurons')\n    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.set_ylabel('neuron')\n    fig.colorbar(im, ax=a)\n\n    # (1,0) inputs for the example trial -------------------------------------------\n    a = ax[1, 0]\n    a.plot(tt, inputs[ti][0], '-o', ms=3, label='time from tone onset (s)')\n    a.plot(tt, inputs[ti][1], '-s', ms=3, label='photostim on')\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', lw=1.5)\n    a.axvline(raw['tone'][ti] - raw['go'][ti], color='g', lw=1.5)\n    a.axhline(0, color='k', lw=0.5)\n    a.set_title('decoder inputs (green = tone onset, red = go cue); input 0 must cross 0 at green')\n    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.legend()\n\n    # (1,1) photostim raster across trials -----------------------------------------\n    a = ax[1, 1]\n    ps = np.array([inputs[t][1] for t in range(n_tr)])\n    a.imshow(ps, aspect='auto', origin='lower', extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, n_tr],\n             cmap='Greys', interpolation='nearest')\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', lw=1.5)\n    a.set_title(f'photostim input, all trials ({int((ps.max(1) > 0).sum())} stim trials)')\n    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.set_ylabel('trial')\n\n    # (2,0) tongue tracking, raw vs binned vs discretised --------------------------\n    a = ax[2, 0]\n    m = (raw['track_ts'] >= raw['go'][ti] + OFF_START) & (raw['track_ts'] < raw['go'][ti] + OFF_END)\n    rel = raw['track_ts'][m] - raw['go'][ti]\n    yy = raw['track_data'][m, 1].copy()\n    vv = raw['track_data'][m, 2] > TONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESH\n    yy[~vv] = np.nan\n    a.plot(rel, yy, '.', ms=3, color='0.5', label='raw visible frames (y)')\n    yb = raw['ty'][ti].copy(); yb[~raw['tvis'][ti]] = np.nan\n    a.step(tt, yb, where='mid', color='tab:blue', label='per-bin mean y')\n    a.axhline(raw['pctl'][0], color='g', ls='--', label='session 40th pct')\n    a.axhline(raw['pctl'][1], color='m', ls='--', label='session 60th pct')\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', lw=1.5)\n    a2 = a.twinx()\n    a2.step(tt, outputs[ti][3], where='mid', color='tab:orange', label='class')\n    a2.set_ylabel('tongue class (0/1/2/3)', color='tab:orange')\n    a.set_title('tongue y-position: raw -> per-bin -> discretised')\n    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.set_ylabel('tongue y (px)'); a.legend(fontsize=7)\n\n    # (2,1) outputs of the example trial -------------------------------------------\n    a = ax[2, 1]\n    for d, nm in enumerate(OUTPUT_NAMES):\n        a.step(tt, outputs[ti][d] + 5 * d, where='mid', label=nm)\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', lw=1.5)\n    a.set_title(f'decoder outputs, trial {ti} '\n                f'(outcome={raw[\"outcome\"][ti]}, instruction={raw[\"instruction\"][ti]})')\n    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.legend(fontsize=8)\n\n    # (3,0) population PSTH split by choice ----------------------------------------\n    a = ax[3, 0]\n    ch = np.array([outputs[t][0][0] for t in range(n_tr)])\n    for c, nm, col in [(0, 'lick left', 'tab:blue'), (1, 'lick right', 'tab:red'),\n                       (2, 'no lick', 'tab:green')]:\n        if (ch == c).sum() < 2:\n            continue\n        psth = np.mean([neural[t].mean(0) for t in np.where(ch == c)[0]], axis=0)\n        a.plot(tt, psth, color=col, label=f'{nm} (n={(ch == c).sum()})')\n    a.axvline(0, color='r', lw=1.5)\n    a.axvline(-1.85, color='g', lw=1, ls='--')\n    a.set_title('population mean firing rate by choice (alignment check)')\n    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); a.set_ylabel('spikes/s'); a.legend()\n\n    # During the sample '\n                'epoch (0.65 s) one of two pure tones (3 kHz / 12 kHz, 3 x 150 ms pulses) '\n                'instructs the animal to lick left or right. After a delay epoch (usually 1.2 s) '\n                'an auditory go cue (6 kHz, 0.1 s) releases the animal, which reports its choice '\n                'by licking one of two lick ports during a 1.5 s answer period; a correct lick is '\n                'rewarded with water. On ~20% of trials ALM was photoinhibited for 0.5 s during '\n                'the delay epoch. Decoded outputs are the lick-direction choice (left/right/no '\n                'lick), the trial outcome (ignore/miss/hit), whether the animal licked early '\n                '(during the sample or delay epoch), and a per-session tertile-style '\n                'discretisation of the tracked tongue y-position (plus a \"not visible\" class).'),\n            'time_bin_size': BIN_SIZE * 1000.0,      # ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'onset of the auditory go cue (t = 0)',\n            'off_start': OFF_START,\n            'off_end': OFF_END,\n            'n_time_bins': N_BINS,\n            'bin_centers_s': BIN_CENTERS.tolist(),\n            'neural_units': 'firing rate, spikes/s (spike count per 50 ms bin / 0.05 s)',\n            'input_descriptions': {\n                'time_from_tone_onset': (\n                    'seconds elapsed since the onset of the instruction tone (sample-epoch start) '\n                    'at each bin centre; negative before the tone. If an early lick triggered a '\n                    'replay of the sample epoch, the last tone before the go cue is used.'),\n                'photostim_on': (\n                    '1 if the 0.5 s ALM photoinhibition stimulus overlapped the bin, else 0.'),\n            },\n            'output_descriptions': {\n                'choice': 'lick direction reported after the go cue, from outcome x instruction',\n                'outcome': 'ignore = no lick in the answer period, miss = incorrect lick, hit = correct lick',\n                'early_lick': 'whether the animal licked during the sample or delay epoch',\n                'tongue_y_position': (\n                    'per-bin mean y-position of the DeepLabCut-tracked tongue (side camera), '\n                    'discretised with the 40th/60th percentiles of the visible bins of that '\n                    'session; class 3 = tongue not visible in the bin'),\n            },\n            'neuron_curation': (\n                \"units labelled 'good' by the region-specific spike-sorting QC classifiers \"\n                \"(units/classification) and having a CCF histology annotation\"),\n            'trial_curation': (\n                'trials covered by the ephys recording (units/obs_intervals and at least one '\n                'spike in the window across all neurons), excluding auto-water and free-water '\n                'trials. Early-lick, no-response and photostimulation trials are kept because '\n                'they are decoder inputs/outputs.'),\n            'session_curation': 'all sessions with at least one good unit and at least 2 trials',\n            'known_limitations': (\n                'The NWB export stores spikes only within [trial start, trial stop]. On error '\n                '(miss) trials the recorded interval ends ~0.8 s after the go cue, so the last '\n                'bins of those trials contain no spikes; 3% of trials likewise lack data before '\n                '~-2.2 s. f'{n[\"n_trials\"]}/{n[\"n_trials_table\"]} trials, '\n              f'{n[\"mean_rate\"]:.2f} spikes/s, {n[\"proc_time\"]:.1f}s  [{el:.0f}s]', flush=True)\n\n\ndef _summary(data):\n    nses = print(f'sessions            : {nses}')\n    print(f'subjects            : {len(data[\"subjects\"])}')\n    print(f'trials (total)      : {sum(ntr)}  (mean {np.mean(ntr):.1f}, '\n          f'range {min(ntr)}-{max(ntr)})')\n    print(f'neurons (total)     : {sum(nn)}  (mean {np.mean(nn):.1f}, range {min(nn)}-{max(nn)})')\n    print(f'time bins per trial : {data[\"neural\"][0][0].shape[1]}')\n                for s in data['input']], axis=1)\n    for i, nm in enumerate(data['input_names']):\n        print(f'input {i} ({nm}): [{allin[i].min():.3f}, {allin[i].max():.3f}]')\n    # output spike times in\n55\t  the DataJoint export are already go-cue-relative, and `align_markers.py` subtracts\n56\t  `go_times` from the video frame times. Our task also aligns to the go cue.\n57\t- **Neuron QC**: `qc_mode='classifier'` → the units labelled `good` by the region-specific\n58\t  logistic-regression classifiers described in the spike-sorting white paper. In the NWB files\n59\t  this is exactly the `units/classification` column (`good` vs `unlabelled`).\n60\t- Units also have to have histology (CCF annotation); the reference intersects ephys unit ids\n61\t  with histology unit ids. In NWB, `units/anno_name` is empty for units without histology.\n62\t- Electrophysiology, so no ΔF/F is needed. Spikes are converted to **firing rates in spikes/s**\n63\t  (`rate=True` in `sliding_histogram`).\n64\t- The reference's 14 brain-area groups are\n65\t  `ALM, Medulla, Midbrain, Striatum, Thalamus, Pons, Cerebellum, Hypothalamus, Hippocampus,\n66\t  Orbital, OtherCortex, Olfactory, CorticalSubplate, Pallidum` (loop in `process_one_sess`).\n67\t- The marker/video analysis keeps `tongue_x`, `tongue_y` from `camera_0_side` — the same stream\n68\t  that the NWB files expose as `acquisition/BehavioralTimeSeries/Camera0_side_TongueTracking`.\n69\t- `generate_5fold_cv_indices.py` additionally drops trials with index < 10 — this is specific to\n70\t  the video-embedding alignment, not to the ephys preprocessing.\n71\t\n72\t---\n73\t\n74\t## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n75\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n76\t\n77\t### Data Structure\n78\t`/app/data/sub-<subject_id>/sub-<subject_id>_ses-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSS>_behavior+ecephys+ogen.nwb`\n79\t(one file per recording session; onset is **relative to trial start**), `task` (always `audio delay`), `task_protocol` (always 1) |\n88\t| `acquisition/BehavioralEvents/*` | `go_start_times`, `sample_start_times`, `delay_start_times`, `presample_*`, `trialend_*`, `left_lick_times`, `right_lick_times`, `photostim_start_times`/`_stop_times`. **`timestamps` are absolute session time (s)**; `data` is a dummy. `go_start_times` has exactly one entry per trial. `sample_start_times` has *more* entries than trials because an early lick triggers a replay of the sample epoch |\n89\t| `acquisition/BehavioralTimeSeries/Camera0_side_{Tongue,Jaw,Nose}Tracking` | `(nframes, 3)` = (`x`, `y`, `likelihood`) from DeepLabCut, `timestamps` absolute session time, frame period 0.0034 s (≈294 Hz). Video is recorded **per trial**: timestamps restart at each trial's `start_time`, with gaps in between |\n90\t| `units/` | `spike_times` (+ `spike_times_index`), `obs_intervals` (+ index), `classification` (`good`/`unlabelled`), `anno_name` (CCF annotation string), `is_good_trials` (n_units × n_trials bool), `electrodes`, 15 QC metrics, waveform metrics |\n91\t| `general/extracellular_ephys/electrodes` | `x`,`y`,`z` = CCF (ML, DV, AP) in µm; `location` = JSON with the **targeted** brain region (`left ALM`, `right Midbrain`, …), `group_name` = probe |\n92\t\n93\t### Dataset Size (from data files)\n94\t| Statistic | Value |\n95\t|-----------|-------|\n96\t| NWB files (sessions) | 174 |\n97\t| Sessions with ≥1 `good` unit | **173** (`sub-440958_ses-20190216T162508` has 0 good units) |\n98\t| Clusters (all) | 272,227 |\n99\t| `good` units (total) | **69,453** (25.5 % of clusters) |\n100\t| `good` units / session | mean 399, min 0, max 923 |\n101\t| Subjects | **28** (4–10 sessions each) |\n102\t| Trials (total, raw) | 94,990 |\n103\t| Trials / session | mean 546, range 264–800 |\n104\t| Probe insertions (unique targets × sessions) | 659 |\n105\t| Unique CCF annotations among good units | 293 |\n106\t\n107\t### Important structural findings (verified by direct inspection)\n108\t1. **Spikes only exist inside `[trial.start_time, trial.stop_time]`.** For every unit tested,\n109\t   100 % of spikes lie inside a trial interval and 0 lie in the inter-trial gaps\n110\t   (`obs_intervals` == the trial intervals). The export is trial-segmented.\n111\t2. `stop_time − go_cue` is **~1.8 s for `hit` and `ignore` trials but only ~0.8 s for `miss`\n112\t   (error) trials** (95 % of miss trials have < 1.5 s of post-go data).\n113\t   `go_cue − start_time` is ≥ 2.5 s for 96.9 % of trials (median 3.15 s).\n114\t3. In 9 sessions the units' `obs_intervals` cover only the **first N trials** of the trials table\n115\t   (e.g. 160 of 480). All units in such a session share exactly the same contiguous trial set —\n116\t   the trials table spans more behaviour than the ephys recording.\n117\t4. `is_good_trials` is not all-True in 4 sessions; however spikes are present on those trials with\n118\t   comparable firing rates (checked in `sub-480135_ses-20210302T140243`: 3.66 vs 3.86 spikes/s),\n119\t   so the flag does *not* indicate missing data.\n120\t5. Tongue `likelihood` is essentially binary (89 % < 0.01, 10.5 % > 0.99). 99.8 % of recorded lick\n121\t   events fall on frames with likelihood > 0.9 → excellent visibility sanity check.\n122\t6. Photostimulation: duration is always 0.5 s; onset is −0.5 s rel. go cue in 122 sessions\n123\t   (late delay) and −1.2 s in 46 sessions (early delay), with a handful of sample-epoch trials.\n124\t   The trials-table values agree with `BehavioralEvents/photostim_{start,stop}_times` to < 10 ms.\n125\t\n126\t---\n127\t\n128\t## Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n129\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n130\t\n131\t### Expected Statistics (from papers/methods)\n132\t| Statistic | Value | Source Quote |\n133\t|-----------|-------|--------------|\n134\t| Good units (total) | 69,943 | \"the dataset consisted of 69,943 good units recorded across 173 behavioral sessions\" (methods.txt) |\n135\t| Sessions | 173 | same |\n136\t| Probe insertions | 655 | \"from which 655 probe insertions were made\" |\n137\t| Fraction of KS2 clusters that are good | 25.9 % | \"This corresponds to 25.9 % of clusters reported by Kilosort2\" |\n138\t| Good units by area | ALM 8717, orbital 10223, striatum 7664, pallidum 1092, thalamus 12808, midbrain 7495, medulla 2928, pons 347, cerebellum 1820, hypothalamus 815, hippocampus 1944, other cortex 7993, olfactory 4137, cortical subplate 1960 | datapaper Fig. 1E / 2F; Keep only classifier-labelled `good` units (`qc_mode='classifier'`).\n160\t2. Keep only units that have histology (non-empty CCF annotation) — units must appear in both the\n161\t   ephys and histology tables.\n162\t3. Assign to one of 14 area groups; units are split by hemisphere at CCF ML = 5700 µm.\n163\t\n164\t**Trial curation rules (reference `get_regular_trial_mask`)**\n165\tno early lick ∧ no auto-water ∧ no free-water ∧ response given ∧ no photostimulation.\n166\t\n167\t### Decoders Trained (accuracies reported in the papers)\n168\t| Decoded variable | Method | Accuracy |\n169\t|---|---|---|\n170\t| Choice, from ALM population (200 neurons, late delay) | logistic regression, 10 ms step | ≈ 0.75–0.85 (Fig. 6D), chance 0.5 |\n171\t| Choice, from thalamus/midbrain/striatum/medulla populations (200 neurons, late delay) | same | ≈ 0.6–0.75 |\n172\t| Choice, from behaviour **video** before sample epoch | CNN embedding | AUC 0.51 ± 0.06 (chance) |\n173\t| Choice, from video, sample+delay epochs | CNN embedding | AUC 0.66 ± 0.12 |\n174\t| Choice, from video, response epoch | CNN embedding | AUC 0.99 ± 0.01 |\n175\t| Choice, from video markers, response epoch | markers | 0.88 ± 0.01 |\n176\t\n177\t→ Expectation for our decoder (all neurons of a session, full −2.5…+1.5 s window): choice should\n178\tbe decoded well above chance, driven mostly by the delay and response epochs.\n179\t\n180\t---\n181\t\n182\t## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n183\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n184\t\n185\t### Discrepancies Found\n186\t| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Papers say | Resolution |\n187\t|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n188\t| Source format | DataJoint `.mat` exports (`neuron_single_units`, `task_cue_time`, …) | NWB (`units/spike_times`, `go_start_times`) | — | The NWB fields map 1-to-1 onto the `.mat` fields used by the reference (see Step 5 table). Spike times are absolute in NWB and go-cue-relative in the `.mat`; we subtract the go-cue time ourselves. |\n189\t| Neuron QC | QC `.mat` files listing \"good units\" per region | `units/classification ∈ {good, unlabelled}` | classifier-based QC, 25.9 % of clusters | Identical concept. Our `good` fraction is 25.5 % (69,453/272,227) — matches. |\n190\t| Region assignment | 14 groups + hemisphere, ALM from a voxel mask (`ALM_voxels_symmetric.npy`, not shipped) | `units/anno_name` = CCF annotation + electrode CCF coordinates | 14 groups with exact unit counts | Built an explicit CCF-annotation → 14-group map (`/app/cache/region_map.py`). It reproduces **11 of the 14 paper counts exactly** (see Step 5 / Step 9). ALM has to be approximated spatially since the voxel mask is missing. |\n191\t| Number of good units | — | 69,453 | 69,943 | 490 fewer (0.7 %). All 490 are in isocortex; every other group matches the paper *exactly*. This is a version difference of the DANDI release (0.230822 vs the version used in the paper). |\n192\t| Sessions | — | 174 files, 173 with good units | 173 sessions | Drop the single session with no good units → exactly 173. |\n193\t| Trials/session | — | mean 546, range 264–800 | mean 476, range 130–785 | The paper's behavioural numbers cover a differently-selected (behaviour-only) session set; the ephys release has more trials per session. Documented, not \"fixed\". |\n194\t| Session selection (perf > 65 %, ≥50 correct L and R) | not implemented in the reference code | 16–23 of the 173 released sessions fail this criterion under every reasonable definition of \"performance\" I tested (mean 0.81, range 0.54–0.97) | performance > 65 %, 84 % mean | The released 173 sessions *are* the 173 sessions the paper reports (exact per-region unit counts). Applying the criterion would make our dataset inconsistent with the reported dataset size, and would bias the required `outcome`/`choice` output distributions. **Decision: keep all 173 sessions**, documented below. |\n195\t| Trial selection | `get_regular_trial_mask` removes early-lick, no-response and photostim trials | — | \"Early lick trials and no response trials were excluded for analysis\" | **Cannot be applied**: `early_lick`, `outcome=ignore`/`choice=no lick` are *required decoder outputs* and photostimulation is a *required decoder input*. Removing them would make three of the four outputs and one of the two inputs constant. We keep them and only apply the parts of the reference mask that do not conflict (auto-water, free-water). This is an explicitly allowed discrepancy. |\n196\t| Photostim epoch | — | late delay (−0.5 s) in 122 sessions, early delay (−1.2 s) in 46 | \"last 0.5 s of the delay epoch\" | Both always end before the go cue, consistent with the paper's statement that photoinhibition never overlaps the response epoch. We simply encode the measured on/off interval. |\n197\t| Bin width | 40 ms / 3.4 ms stride | — | 40 ms (also 20/80/160/320 ms; 200 ms for decoding) | The task specification mandates **50 ms** bins; we use 50 ms non-overlapping bins. Allowed discrepancy (decoder-task requirement); the paper shows results are robust across bin widths 20–320 ms. |\n198\t\n199\t---\n200\t\n201\t## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n202\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n203\t\n204\t### Variable Mapping\n205\t| Source (NWB) | Reference `.mat` equivalent | Target field | Transform |\n206\t|---|---|---|---|\n207\t| `units/spike_times` (+ `spike_times_index`) | `neuron_single_units` | `neural` | subtract go-cue time; count spikes in 80 non-overlapping 50 ms bins spanning [−2.5, +1.5) s; divide by 0.05 → spikes/s (`sliding_histogram(..., rate=True)`) |\n208\t| `units/classification == 'good'` | QC `Idx` good-unit lists | neuron filter | keep `good` only |\n209\t| `units/anno_name` | `histology.annotation` (`ccf_label`) | `brain_region_idx` | CCF annotation → one of 14 groups |\n210\t| `electrodes/x,y,z` via `units/electrodes` | `histology.ccf_x/y/z` | ALM assignment / hemisphere | x = ML (midline 5700 µm), y = DV, z = AP (bregma at z = 5400 µm) |\n211\t| `acquisition/BehavioralEvents/go_start_times/timestamps` | `task_cue_time[0]` | alignment event | t = 0 for every trial |\n212\t| `acquisition/BehavioralEvents/sample_start_times/timestamps` | `task_sample_time[0]` | `input[0]` | last sample-epoch start before the go cue = **tone onset**; `input[0][t] = t_bin_center − t_tone` (s) |\n213\t| `intervals/trials/photostim_onset`,`photostim_duration` | `task_stimulation[:,2:4]` | `input[1]` | binary: 1 if the bin overlaps `[start_time+onset, +duration]`, else 0 |\n214\t| `intervals/trials/outcome` + `trial_instruction` | `behavior_report` + `task_trial_type` | `output[0]` (`choice`) | hit → instruction; miss → opposite of instruction; ignore → `no lick` |\n215\t| `intervals/trials/outcome` | `behavior_report` | `output[1]` (`outcome`) | ignore→0, miss→1, hit→2 |\n216\t| `intervals/trials/early_lick` | `behavior_early_report` | `output[2]` (`early_lick`) | `no early`→0, `early`→1 |\n217\t| `BehavioralTimeSeries/Camera0_side_TongueTracking` | `tracking.camera_0_side.tongue_y` / `..._likelihood` | `output[3]` (`tongue_y_position`) | per bin: visible if any frame with likelihood > 0.5; y = mean y of visible frames; discretise by session percentiles (40th, 60th) of the visible per-bin y; not visible → 3 |\n218\t| `intervals/trials/auto_water`, `free_water` | `behavior_is_auto_water`, `behavior_is_free_water` | trial filter | exclude |\n219\t| `units/obs_intervals` | — | trial filter | keep only trials covered by the ephys recording |\n220\t| `general/subject/description` (`SC015`) | mouse | `subjects` | mouse name |\n221\t\n222\t### Target structure\n223\t- `input_names = ['time_from_tone_onset', 'photostim_on']` — both `(2, 80)` per trial.\n224\t- `output_names = ['choice', 'outcome', 'early_lick', 'tongue_y_position']` — `(4, 80)` per trial\n225\t  (the three per-trial variables are broadcast over time, as the spec asks for time-varying\n226\t  outputs \"if at all possible\").\n227\t- `output_values = [['left','right','no lick'], ['ignore','miss','hit'], ['no','yes'],\n228\t  ['<40th pct','40th-60th pct','>60th pct','not visible']]`.\n229\t- `brain_regions` = the 14 paper groups.\n230\t\n231\t### Key Decisions\n232\t1. **Alignment = go-cue onset, window [−2.5, +1.5) s, 80 × 50 ms non-overlapping bins.**\n233\t   Mandated by the task; the reference also aligns everything to the go cue. Bin *edges* are\n234\t   `−2.5 + 0.05·k`; a spike is in bin k if `t ∈ [edge_k, edge_{k+1})`, matching the half-open\n235\t   convention of the reference `sliding_histogram`.\n236\t2. **Neural values are firing rates in spikes/s** (`count / 0.05`), as in the reference\n237\t   (`rate=True`).\n238\t3. **Neuron curation = classifier `good` + has a CCF annotation.** This is exactly the\n239\t   reference's QC. No additional firing-rate threshold: the paper only applies a 2 Hz threshold\n240\t   inside one specific AUC analysis, not to the dataset.\n241\t4. **Session curation**: keep the 173 sessions that contain ≥ 1 good unit. The behavioural\n242\t   selection criterion from the paper (perf > 65 %, ≥ 50 correct L/R) is *not* applied because\n243\t   (a) the released 173 sessions are demonstrably the 173 sessions whose unit counts the paper\n244\t   reports, and (b) it would bias the required `choice`/`outcome` distributions.\n245\t5. **Trial curation**:\n246\t   - exclude trials not covered by the ephys recording (`obs_intervals`);\n247\t   - exclude `auto_water` and `free_water` trials (reference `get_regular_trial_mask`; these are\n248\t     not genuine behavioural reports);\n249\t   - **keep** early-lick, no-response (`ignore`) and photostimulation trials — required by the\n250\t     decoder input/output specification. This is the one deliberate departure from\n251\t     `get_regular_trial_mask`.\n252\t6. **Missing post-go data on error trials.** Because the export is trial-segmented, ~95 % of\n253\t   `miss` trials have no spikes after ≈ +0.8 s, and 3 % of trials have no spikes before\n254\t   ≈ −2.2 s. Those bins get a firing rate of 0. The alternative — dropping every trial whose\n255\t   window is not fully covered — would delete 95 % of all `miss` trials and make the `outcome`\n256\t   output nearly degenerate, so it is rejected. The consequence (late-window zeros are\n257\t   informative about `outcome`) is quantified in Step 10/12.\n258\t7. **Tone onset** = the *last* sample-epoch start before the go cue. Early licks during the\n259\t   sample/delay epoch trigger a replay of the epoch, so a trial can contain several tone\n260\t   presentations; the final one is the instruction the animal must report, and it is always\n261\t   1.85 s before the go cue (median). Earlier replays fall outside or at the edge of the window.\n262\t8. **Photostimulation input** from the trials table (`start_time + photostim_onset`, duration),\n263\t   cross-validated against `BehavioralEvents/photostim_*_times` (agreement < 10 ms).\n264\t9. **Choice** derived from `outcome` × `trial_instruction` rather than from raw lick times: this\n265\t   is the experiment's own definition (`behavior_report` in the reference), is exact, and does not\n266\t   depend on a lick-detection heuristic. Validated against the lick event streams (Step 10).\n267\t10. **Tongue visibility threshold 0.5** on the DeepLabCut likelihood. The likelihood is bimodal\n268\t    (89 % < 0.01, 10.5 % > 0.99) so any threshold in (0.01, 0.99) gives the same answer\n269\t    (10.586 % vs 10.517 % of frames for 0.5 vs 0.9).\n270\t11. **Tongue percentiles are computed per session over the *visible* per-bin y values** inside the\n271\t    extracted windows. y for non-visible frames is meaningless (ranges −5…364 vs 239…327 when\n272\t    visible), so including it would corrupt the percentiles.\n273\t12. **Brain regions**: 14 paper groups, hemisphere-agnostic (the paper's Fig. 2F counts are\n274\t    hemisphere-agnostic and we reproduce them exactly). ALM is defined as units annotated in\n275\t    the somatomotor areas (MOp/MOs) or frontal pole whose CCF AP coordinate is ≥ 2.0 mm anterior\n276\t    to bregma (z ≤ 3400 µm); this approximates the reference's missing ALM voxel mask and yields\n277\t    8,464 units vs the paper's 8,717 in a dataset with 490 fewer cortical units.\n278\t\n279\t### Planned Sanity Checks\n280\t- [x] Per-region good-unit counts vs the paper's 14 numbers (Fig. 2F).\n281\t- [x] Total good units / clusters ratio vs 25.9 %.\n282\t- [x] Number of sessions (173) and subjects (28).\n283\t- [x] Probe insertions (659 vs 655).\n284\t- [x] Tongue likelihood high at lick times (99.8 %).\n285\t- [x] Photostim trials-table timings == BehavioralEvents timings.\n286\t- [ ] Choice derived from outcome×instruction == side of the licks recorded after the go cue.\n287\t- [ ] Spike counts recomputed directly from the raw file for random (session, trial, neuron, bin).\n288\t- [ ] Tone-onset input == 0 exactly 1.85 s before the go cue for a typical trial.\n289\t- [ ] Photostim input on exactly during `[onset, onset+0.5 s]`.\n290\t- [ ] Tongue-y class fractions ≈ 40/20/40 of the visible bins.\n291\t- [ ] Mean session firing rate in a plausible range (1–10 spikes/s).\n292\t\n293\t---\n294\t\n295\t## Step 6: Script Development\n296\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n297\t\n298\t`/app/convert_data.py` — runs as\n299\t`python -u /app/convert_data.py <outpicklefile> [--full|--sample] [--show-processing] [--workers N]`.\n300\t\n301\tStructure:\n302\t| Function | Purpose |\n303\t|---|---|\n304\t| `annotation_to_group` / `assign_brain_regions` | CCF annotation (+ CCF AP coordinate for ALM) → one of the 14 brain-area groups |\n305\t| `bin_spikes` | go-cue-aligned 50 ms spike-rate binning; one `np.searchsorted` of all `(n_trials × 81)` bin edges per unit |\n306\t| `tongue_per_bin` | per-bin mean tongue y over the DeepLabCut frames with likelihood > 0.5, and a per-bin visibility flag |\n307\t| `discretize_tongue` | 40th/60th-percentile discretisation over the visible bins of the session |\n308\t| `process_session` | loads one NWB file, curates units and trials, builds `neural`/`input`/`output` |\n309\t| `make_processing_plot` | 8-panel diagnostic figure per session (`--show-processing`) |\n310\t| `main` / `_worker` | multiprocessing driver, assembly of the final dict, summary printout |\n311\t\n312\tEfficiency notes:\n313\t- **Identified inefficiency**: a naive per-trial/per-unit loop over spikes would be\n314\t  O(n_units × n_trials × n_spikes). Replaced by one `np.searchsorted` per unit over the\n315\t  flattened `(n_trials, 81)` edge array → O(n_units · n_trials · 81 · log n_spikes).\n316\t- **Identified inefficiency**: `h5py` per-unit slicing of `units/spike_times` issues\n317\t  hundreds of small reads. The dataset is read **once** into memory per session (≤ 300 MB)\n318\t  and sliced in numpy.\n319\t- **Speed-up**: 16 worker processes (`multiprocessing`, spawn context), one session each.\n320\t- Neural data is stored as `float32`; outputs as `int64`; inputs as `float32`.\n321\t- Per-session and cumulative timing is printed for every session.\n322\t\n323\tRobustness / edge cases handled:\n324\t- sessions with 0 good units (1 session) → dropped;\n325\t- units whose `obs_intervals` cover only part of the trials table (9 sessions) → only the\n326\t  covered trials are kept;\n327\t- one extra trial listed in `obs_intervals` beyond the end of the spike record (2 sessions)\n328\t  → detected as \"no spike from any of ≥ 90 neurons in the whole 4 s window\" and dropped;\n329\t- trials with `photostim_onset == 'N/A'` → zero photostim input;\n330\t- sessions with no visible tongue at all → all bins class 3 (percentiles undefined);\n331\t- assertions on: one go cue per trial, a tone onset before every go cue, known outcome labels.\n332\t\n333\t---\n334\t\n335\t## Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n336\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n337\t\n338\tCommand: `python -u /app/convert_data.py /app/sample_data.pkl --sample --show-processing`\n339\t(→ `/app/conversion_sample_out.txt`), then\n340\t`python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/sample_data.pkl --verify-only`\n341\t(→ `/app/verification_sample_out.txt`).\n342\t\n343\t### Sample Statistics (2 sessions, mouse SC015)\n344\t| Statistic | Value |\n345\t|-----------|-------|\n346\t| Sessions | 2 |\n359\t\n360\tThe tongue classes 0:1:2 are in the expected 2:1:2 ratio (40 %/20 %/40 % of the *visible*\n361\tbins), confirming the percentile discretisation.\n362\t\n363\t### Processing Plots Review\n364\t`processing_SC015_20190207_120657_s1.png`, `processing_SC015_20190208_133600_s2.png`:\n365\t1. Raw spike ticks fall exactly under the corresponding binned-rate steps — no temporal\n366\t   shift, no off-by-one bin.\n367\t2. `time_from_tone_onset` crosses zero exactly at the plotted tone-onset marker.\n368\t3. `photostim_on` is exactly 1 during the plotted stimulation interval (11 bins = 0.55 s\n369\t   for a 0.5 s stimulus that straddles bin boundaries) and 0 elsewhere; the all-trial\n370\t   photostim raster shows the expected [−1.2, −0.7] s (and, in a few trials, sample-epoch)\n371\t   blocks, always ending before the go cue.\n372\t4. Tongue: raw per-frame y values, the per-bin mean, the session 40th/60th percentile lines\n373\t   and the resulting class labels are mutually consistent.\n374\t5. Population PSTH aligned to the go cue shows the expected sharp go-cue-locked response and\n375\t   a left/right divergence after the go cue — confirms the alignment.\n376\t6. Tongue visibility is ~2 % before the go cue and jumps to ~80 % just after it.\n377\t\n378\t### Issue found and fixed\n379\tThe first sample run produced a warning `Session 1, trial 159: all neural data is zero`.\n380\tInvestigation: `units/obs_intervals` of `SC015_20190208_133600` lists 160 trials, but the\n381\tlast spike in the file is at t = 1107.44 s while trial 159's window starts at 1110.14 s —\n382\tthe observation table over-reports coverage by one trial. | Stream | Check | Result |\n510\t|---|--------|-------|--------|\n511\t| 1 | neural | For 6 random sessions × 4 random (trial, neuron, bin): spike count recomputed with a boolean mask `(st >= go+edge_k) & (st < go+edge_{k+1})` equals `converted × 0.05` | 24/24 exact |\n512\t| 1 | neural | For 6 random sessions × 1 trial: total spikes of *all* neurons in `[go−2.5, go+1.5)` equals `sum(neural)×0.05` | 6/6 exact |\n513\t| 1 | neural | number of neurons and trials per session equals the independently recomputed good-unit / kept-trial counts | 6/6 |\n514\t| 2 | input | `time_from_tone_onset` recomputed from a python `max(x for x in sample_starts if x < go)` | 24/24, max abs diff 2e-7 |\n515\t| 2 | input | `photostim_on` recomputed with an explicit per-bin overlap loop from the trials table | 24/24 exact |\n516\t| 3 | output | `outcome`, `early_lick`, `choice` recomputed from the trials table for **every trial** of 8 random sessions | 24/24 sessions-columns exact |\n517\t| 3 | output | `choice` cross-validated against the **raw lick event streams**: side of the first lick in `[go, go+1.5]` | 3,669/3,674 lick trials, 447/449 no-lick trials |\n518\t| 3 | output | `tongue_y_position` recomputed from the raw DeepLabCut stream with `np.digitize` binning and independently computed percentiles, **all** trials × 80 bins of 8 sessions | 0 mismatching bins of 336,000 |\n519\t| 4 | regions | per-area good-unit counts vs data-paper Fig. 2F | 11/14 exact, 3 within 3 % |\n520\t| 5 | bookkeeping | 173 sessions, 28 subjects, `subject_idx` → correct mouse, `len(brain_region_idx)==n_neurons`, every trial 80 bins, `float32`, no negative rates | all pass |\n521\t\n522\t**The 5 `choice`-vs-first-lick disagreements were investigated individually.** All five are\n523\t`ignore` trials that contain exactly one lick 1–47 ms after the go cue (e.g.\n524\t`SC060_20210322_164725_s4` trial 467: a single right lick at +0.007 s). (In this release all 69,453 `good` units already have an annotation, so the histology term removes nothing.) Hemisphere is recorded in `session_info` but not used to split `brain_regions`, because the paper's published counts are hemisphere-agnostic. |\n536\t| (c) **Temporal alignment** | go cue = 0 (`task_cue_time[0]`); spikes truncated to `[begin_time, end_time]` around it; video markers aligned by `times_for_frames − go_times` | go cue = `go_start_times` timestamp; spike times and video timestamps both have the go-cue time subtracted | ✅ identical |\n537\t| (d) **Binning** | `sliding_histogram`: bins `[c−bw/2, c+bw/2)`, rate = count/bw; paper used bw = 40 ms, stride 3.4 ms | non-overlapping bins `[edge_k, edge_{k+1})`, rate = count/0.05 s | ⚠ **50 ms / stride 50 ms instead of 40 ms / 3.4 ms — mandated by the decoder-task specification.** Same half-open convention and the same spikes→rate conversion. The data paper shows results are stable for bin widths 20–320 ms. |\n538\t| (e) **Input construction** | The reference builds no decoder inputs; it does keep `task_sample_time`, `task_cue_time` and `task_stimulation` (laser on/off times, referenced to the go cue) | `time_from_tone_onset` from `sample_start_times`; `photostim_on` from `photostim_onset/duration` — the NWB equivalents of `task_sample_time` and `task_stimulation[:,2:4]` | ✅ same source variables |\n539\t| (f) **Output construction** | `behavior_report` (correctness), `task_trial_type` (instruction), `behavior_early_report`, `tracking.camera_0_side.tongue_y` | `outcome`, `trial_instruction`, `early_lick`, `Camera0_side_TongueTracking` | ✅ same source variables. `choice` = `correctness × trial_type` is the same construction the reference uses for its `<stimulus, choice>` trial types. |\n540\t| (g) **Trial curation** | `get_regular_trial_mask`: no early lick ∧ no auto-water ∧ no free-water ∧ response given ∧ no photostim | no auto-water ∧ no free-water ∧ ephys coverage | ⚠ **deliberate**: early-lick, no-response and photostim trials are the decoder's own targets/inputs and cannot be removed. Everything else matches. |\n541\t| (h) **Session curation** | none in the code | ≥ 1 good unit, ≥ 2 trials | ✅ (see Step 4 for why the paper's behavioural session criterion is not applied) |\n542\t| (i) **Extra reference step not used** | `generate_5fold_cv_indices.py` drops trials with index < 10 | not applied | justified: that rule belongs to the video-embedding alignment pipeline, not the ephys preprocessing; the first trials are already removed when they are free-water/auto-water trials. |\n543\t\n544\t### Check 4 — Key statistics vs the papers\n545\tSee the table in Step 9. Alternative criteria (a 0.75 mm cylinder around AP 2.5/ML 1.5 → 8,362 units;\n572\t   ML ≥ 1.0 mm & AP ≥ 1.5 mm → 8,427) give similar numbers; the chosen rule lands at 8,464,\n573\t   i.e. 8,717 × (16,220/16,710) = 8,462 expected after accounting for the missing units.\n574\t\n575\t### Check 5 — Edge cases\n576\t| Edge case | Handling / verification |\n577\t|---|---|\n578\t| Bin boundaries | half-open `[edge_k, edge_{k+1})`; a spike exactly at the go cue lands in bin 50 (`t ∈ [0, 0.05)`). The provided decoder is a **per-timepoint linear model** (`nn.Linear(nneurons, npcs)` → `nn.Linear(npcs+dinput, nclasses)`, `decoder.py:942,988`) with no temporal context, so it can only exploit this on the 3.2 % of bins that are actually empty. Measured directly in Step 12: held-out `outcome` balanced accuracy is 0.659 over all bins and **0.652 over the 96.8 % of bins where the recording is still live** — the artifact is worth only 0.007. |\n590\t\n591\t### Issues found and resolved in this step\n592\t- **All-zero trial** (found in Step 7): fixed, see above; re-ran conversion, verification,\n593\t  sanity checks and statistics — all clean.\n594\t- **Choice-vs-lick check threshold**: the original majority-lick criterion gave 92–98 %\n595\t  agreement; switching to the (behaviourally correct) first-lick criterion gave 100 % apart\n596\t  from 5 boundary-artifact trials. in absolute terms it\n644\tis the best-decoded output, and time-resolved it reaches 0.84 during the delay epoch — the\n645\tepoch in which an early lick actually happens.\n646\t\n647\t### Check 2 — Accuracy comparison to the papers\n648\tThe papers report decoding **as a function of time**, so the comparison is made per epoch.\n649\tOur choice accuracy is restricted to left/right trials to make it the same 2-class,\n650\tchance-0.5 problem the papers report.\n651\t\n652\t| Quantity | Paper value | Ours (held-out) | Comment |\n653\t|---|---|---|---|\n654\t| Choice decoding, **pre-sample** epoch | AUC 0.51 ± 0.06 (video embedding, n=106 sessions); at chance for neural populations (Fig. 6D) | **0.545** | ✅ near chance, as expected — the instruction has not been given yet |\n655\t| Choice decoding, **sample** epoch | AUC 0.66 ± 0.12 (video, sample+delay) | **0.670** | ✅ |\n656\t| Choice decoding, **delay** epoch | ALM pseudo-population of 200 neurons, late delay: ≈ 0.75–0.85; thalamus/midbrain/striatum/medulla ≈ 0.6–0.75 (Fig. 6D) | **0.713** | ✅ our value pools *all* 14 areas and all sessions, so it should sit between ALM (highest) and the weakly-selective areas (hippocampus, olfactory, other cortex), which is exactly where it lands |\n657\t| Choice decoding, **response** epoch | markers 0.88 ± 0.01;
